W4.A0: every failed REAL run (450 across manuk, manuk+rollback, cdp-layer, playwright) now gets one primary cause from its transcript; per-arm table in docs/sprint/w4/A0.md, but the labels do not yet pass their hand-check, so the node is BLOCKED | gate: hand-label sample of 10 failed runs must agree with bench/agent-tasks/real_classify.py at >=9/10 | evidence: fresh sample 2 agreed 5/10 (sample 1, dev, 5/10 before refinements); table counts manuk 132 failed (typed_not_registered 47, dialog_modal 40, key_hover_enter_gap 18), cdp-layer 116 (handle_lost 39, dialog_modal 33, wrong_answer 18); chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116
W3.12: act/type/navigate results now end with a settle line from the engine's own queues (settled: 0 fetch, 0 timers<=2s, layout clean | pending: N fetch (path), M timers<=2s; later timers named, not waited on); click->fetch(400ms)->setTimeout(1500)->render lands in the first observation on both backends, a 10 s timer is reported pending; Readiness carries Pending | gate: mcp/tests/settle.rs on manuk + chrome backends, red under MANUK_SETTLE_MUTATE=no-settle (both fail) | evidence: engine act 415 ms wall, chrome 2.2 s, 2/2 pass, mutant 0/2; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; deterministic <150 ms wall not separately measured (W3.12.a)
W4.A1: engine `type` now focuses the field and fires per-character keydown/beforeinput/input/keyup then change, so React-style controlled inputs keep typed text on both backends (live opendining/staynb re-check is W3.18.a.a.a) | gate: mcp/tests/controlled_input.rs (manuk+chrome) and engine/page/tests/g_type_text*.rs; reverting type_into_handle to the attribute-only write turns the manuk-backend test red | evidence: old path FAILED manuk test, new path 2/2 pass, page tests 2/2; verify.sh all gates green (706s)
BADGRAPH row 203: 'W4.A1.a': text a page timer writes with textContent (setInterval render) now shows in extract/observe text instead of vanishing | gate: engine/page/tests/timer_text.rs (settle then visible_text contains tick) | evidence: red without the fix (visible_text ""), green with it; MCP probe extract returned 'tick1589'; cause: relayout's 'tree grew' restyle check compared node count, textContent swaps a text node so count was equal and the new node had no style; now membership check; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116
W3.18.a.a.a: opendining's reservation form now accepts phone numbers and completes under manuk (React onChange fires: engine exposes on* event-handler IDL attributes, so React no longer falls back to its IE9 input polyfill) | gate: real_run.py opendining-3/4/5/8/10 x3, 2/3 rule, >=3 of 5 | evidence: 0/15 runs before; after: opendining-10 3/3, -8 3/3, -5 2/3 pass (3 of 5 tasks), -3 1/3, -4 0/3; engine/page/tests/g_event_handler_idl_defaults.rs red without fix; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116
W3.8: agents can now press keys (Escape closes a modal, ArrowDown+Enter drives a combobox), upload a file, answer a native confirm(), follow an OAuth popup and back, and download a file into a granted dir (0600, 100 MB cap, name/bytes/sha256/MIME reported) | gate: mcp/tests/write_primitives.rs, 11 tests x3 runs on manuk+chrome (chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116) | evidence: 3/3 green (11 passed, ~49 s each); mutants red: key dispatch removed -> Escape/combobox/confirm tests fail, confirm default=accept -> unarmed-confirm test fails, cap 4000 MB -> 200 MB download test fails; 200 MB response aborted at 104857600 bytes, nothing kept; chrome-only for confirm/popup/download because the engine has none of those; drag untested (W3.8.a); mcp/tests/cdp.rs has 4 reds that pre-date this (W3.8.b)
W4.A4: observe lines now name the card/list they sit in (3 identical 'Add to cart' buttons are told apart), mark script/onclick-only divs [clickable], keep handles across pushState routes and re-renders (re-bound by role+name+container), select ARIA comboboxes (open, pick, verify), keep content text before chrome text when over budget | gate: mcp/tests/spa.rs on manuk+chrome backends; mutants TREE=flat, REMOUNT, FIT=chrome-first each go red | evidence: spa 3/3 pass, mcp+agent suites green; engine sees onclick/tabindex only (W4.A4.a), engine tree has no paragraph text so budget policy is chrome-backend only (W4.A4.b), cdp stale-scroll click (W4.A4.c); chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116
W4.A5: fill_form(fields[], submit) and act(steps[]) run a whole form in ONE tool call on both backends and stop at the first failed step, dialog, popup or navigation (reporting which step); back and wait_for(text|handle, timeout) added, timeout is a clean TIMED OUT result | gate: mcp/tests/batch.rs (11 tests, manuk+chrome), mutant 'never stop on navigation' turns navigation_stops_the_batch_* red | evidence: 11/11 pass; 6-field form (text, select, checkbox, radio, submit) = 1 call vs 6; engine select-option click gap and 4 pre-existing cdp.rs failures logged as W4.A5.a/.b
W4.A6: re-bench after the layer fixes. Engine (manuk) REAL 11/47 and cdp-layer REAL 15/47 (up from 10/47); cdp-layer beats manuk on REAL, so any gain is a LAYER gain, not an engine win. Manuk own suite x3 is 38/39/38, outside the 39-40 bar. | gate: REAL cdp-layer >=4 tasks outside band over 10/47 (MET); own-suite manuk within 39-40 (NOT MET: F08 3/3, L12 2/3 fail) | evidence: docs/sprint/w4/A6-real.tsv (141 rows, chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116, 3 runs each for manuk and cdp-layer, 1 for playwright 25/47); own suite rows in docs/sprint/SCOREBOARD.tsv 2026-10-31; addendum in docs/sprint/VERDICT.md; node BLOCKED, follow-up W4.A6.a.
R2.1.5.a.a: manuk-mcp extract is capped at 20000 chars per result with an offset cursor ("more: call extract again with offset=N"), so a large Wikipedia page no longer spills to a file the agent must Read back | gate: L18+L21 manuk arm 1 run each: zero Read calls, extract results 20275/20276 chars (spilled before), both PASS; mcp test extract_pages_a_long_page_with_a_cursor goes red when cap is 20M (92500-char result) | evidence: cache_create L18 28.8k (was 59.1k), L21 38.9k (was 78.6k), single runs not a band; 4 pre-existing cdp test failures queued as R2.1.5.a.a.a; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116
W4.B1: manuk loads navigate/act/type/extract upfront (0 ToolSearch turns, was 115 without it); per task manuk 6.17→4.97 turns and 705→553 output tokens (−22%), pass 115→116 of 120 runs | gate: unit core_tools_are_always_loaded red on mutation; live mutation 2 ToolSearch turns vs 0 on F01+L01; own suite x3 per arm, chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116, suite 69903c7d | evidence: manuk 120 runs 4.97 turns, 563 in+out, 39/40 tasks 2-of-3 pass; manuk-prealwaysload 6.17 turns, 716 in+out, 38/40; cdp-layer 3.40 turns, 385 in+out, 40/40; playwright 5.97 turns, 589 in+out, 39/40; playwright+preload (ENABLE_TOOL_SEARCH=false) 4.96 turns, 414 in+out, 39/40. W4.C4: every act result now lists what it wrote (storage keys + non-safe requests, or 'writes: none'), so an agent can verify an add-to-cart really landed | gate: cargo test -p manuk-mcp --test receipts + c4_receipts.py (receipts lift verifier accuracy >=20pt; mutation red) | evidence: manuk 96% vs 42% without receipts, cdp-layer 83% vs 38%, playwright 42%; cdp-layer ties so LAYER win; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116

W4.E3.a: with MANUK_HTTP_CACHE_DIR set, page <script> bodies are compiled once and later visits decode a stored SpiderMonkey stencil instead of re-parsing (key = sha256(build id, source name, body); corrupt or stale files fall back to a recompile) | gate: scripts/w4e3a-gate.py, 3 pages x 3 runs, warm max < cold min and a11y digest equal; key-without-build-id mutant red | evidence: script ms cold->warm three 28.3-28.5 -> 16.1-16.5, ethers 42.9-43.1 -> 15.1-15.3, axe 52.4-53.5 -> 40.0-41.1 (local copies, chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116)
W4.E3.b: manuk-mcp --http-cache-dir <dir> turns on the disk HTTP cache, which now also caches responses with Last-Modified and no explicit lifetime (10% heuristic); on books.toscrape.com a warm visit serves its subresources from disk, and the confined process can write the cache dir (before, every write was EACCES and the cache stayed empty) | gate: scripts/w4e3b-gate.py, 3 runs, warm wire below the 700055 B band and below cold, a11y text equal; heuristic-disabled mutant -> warm 700055, gate red | evidence: cold wire 792684 B -> warm 0 B in 3 of 3 runs (35 cache entries), a11y identical; books warm wire band in W4.E3 was 700055; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116 (follow-ups: W4.E3.b.a wire accounting, W4.E3.b.b exit segfault)

R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a: BLOCKED, nothing new shipped: ebanking.bdm-sa.com refuses manuk but not openssl or Chrome, so the failure is a manuk ClientHello difference rather than host-wide drops | gate: manuk EOF only when openssl also has no cert (>=20 interleaved probes) | evidence: 20 rounds, openssl cert in 10, manuk rc=1 in 20/20 (10/10 with cert), Chrome 145 dump-dom 20/20 at 187986 B; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116 (docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-PAIRED-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv); follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: BLOCKED, nothing new shipped: ebanking.bdm-sa.com sends its server flight for only ~20-40% of hellos and then closes, for manuk and openssl alike, so no single ClientHello field explains manuk's failure | gate: a11y-dump 5/5 exit 0 after a hello change (not reachable: no field found; no mutant needed) | evidence: relay logs manuk 12/30 flights seen 0 full handshakes, openssl GET 0/6 HTTP, Chrome 145 empty DOM 3/3 in same window; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116 (docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-HELLO-BISECT-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv); follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: nothing new works; BLOCKED. The Chrome 'success' we compared against is HTTP/3 over QUIC on the default profile, not a TCP hello, so no ClientHello bisect can match it | gate: >50000 B on 3 of 3 runs in a window (met on default profile: 187986 B x3); relay logs of the server flight + FIN; flag bisection to a failing Chrome (not reached: default Chrome never fails; fresh-profile Chrome fails 0 B x3 at 90 s) | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-CHROME-WINDOW-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv; netlog protocol_str=quic x3 default, h2 with --disable-quic; relay 0 B S>C on 6/6 conns; manuk a11y-dump rc=0 3/3 in same window; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: a like-for-like ebanking check now exists: fresh TCP-only Chrome and manuk run back to back, and the Chrome row is rejected if its netlog shows QUIC. In these windows manuk loads the BDM login page (accessibility tree, 3228 B) and fresh-profile TCP Chrome does not (0 B), so the earlier "Chrome succeeds" comparison was HTTP/3 only | gate: red mutant (default profile, QUIC netlog) rejected by the checker, plus 3 back-to-back windows | evidence: M0 mutant netlog protocol_str=quic x4 -> REJECTED; W1-W3 Chrome --disable-quic 0 B rc=124 at 120 s, netlog h2,http/1.1 (valid); manuk rc=0 3228 B at 9, 12, 7 s; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116 (docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-ORACLE-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv); follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking.bdm-sa.com does not reliably load from manuk. Of 10 interleaved rounds in one window, the first 3 load the BDM login page (3228 B) and the next 7 fail (rc=1, 0 B), so the 7/7 bar stays unmet and the earlier 3/3 pass was not stable | gate: 10 interleaved rounds, failed = manuk rc!=0, plus red mutant (bad host, 10/10 rows recorded failed) | evidence: W1 rounds 1-3 rc=0 3228 B at 7-8 s, rounds 4-10 rc=1 at 15-18 s; openssl cert=1 in 6/10, rc=1 in 8/10; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116 (docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-INTERLEAVE-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv); follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking spacing check done, no spacing rule: in one window, probes 10 s apart failed 7 of 10 and probes 120 s apart failed 5 of 10, and both blocks went fail-then-pass rather than pass-then-fail, so spacing does not explain the earlier pattern | gate: red mutant (127.0.0.1:9, 10 probes) recorded 10/10 failed, both blocks 10 rows each | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-SPACING-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv; mutant 10/10 rc!=0; A 3/10 pass, B 5/10 pass, Fisher p=0.65; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking.bdm-sa.com loaded in all 40 probes of one 25-minute window (3228 B each), so the fail-then-pass switch did not recur and no elapsed-time rule is supported | gate: red mutant (127.0.0.1:9, 10 probes) recorded 10/10 failed, plus 40 probes 30 s apart in one window | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-CLOCK-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv; mutant 10/10 rc!=0; block C 40/40 rc=0, 0 failed, first pass at probe 1; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking.bdm-sa.com still fails from manuk in a fresh window: 3 of 10 probes load the BDM login page (3228 B) and 7 fail with "tls handshake eof", so the 40/40 window was the exception, not the rule | gate: red mutant (127.0.0.1:9, 10 probes) recorded 10/10 failed, plus 10 probes 10 s apart against ebanking in one fresh window | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-FRESH-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv; mutant 10/10 rc=1 (connection refused); C rc=0 x3 then rc=1 x7 (first 3 pass, rest fail, 14-17 s); chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking.bdm-sa.com in one window: manuk loads the BDM login page in 3 of 10 probes (3228 B) and fails the other 7 with a TLS handshake EOF; the failing probes show a partial server flight (8 TLS records) in 3 cases and no server bytes in 4, and no rule explains the split | gate: red mutant (127.0.0.1:9, 10 probes) recorded 10/10 failed, plus 10 manuk probes each paired with openssl s_client -msg, 10 s apart | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-HS-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv; mutant 10/10 rc=1; C pass rows 3/3 server_msgs 13,13,8; fail rows server_msgs 8,8,8 (3) and 0,0,0,0 (4); Fisher two-sided p=0.5; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking.bdm-sa.com from manuk, own rustls trace: all 7 failing probes make 9 internal TLS attempts, in 6 of 7 at least one attempt parses ServerHello+cert+ECDHE (stage 4) yet the probe ends with no page; the 3 passing probes succeed on the first attempt (3228 B); stage 4 in failing vs passing rows is not different (6/7 vs 3/3, Fisher p=1), no rule adopted | gate: red mutant (127.0.0.1:9, 10 probes) recorded 10/10 failed with 0 attempts, plus 10 manuk probes with RUST_LOG=rustls=trace 10 s apart | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-HS2-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv; C rc=1 x7 (attempts 9, max_stage 4,4,4,4,4,4,0) then rc=0 x3 (attempts 1); stage = rustls log markers not per-record counts, so openssl 8-record figure is not directly comparable; chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: ebanking.bdm-sa.com, per-attempt rustls trace in one window (hs3-w1): 9 of 10 manuk probes fail and each failing probe makes 9 internal TLS attempts; the one pass (3228 B) needed 7 attempts, so 'passes succeed on attempt 1' from the HS2 note does not hold here; stage 4 (ServerHello+cert+ECDHE parsed) occurs in some attempt of 9/9 failing and 1/1 passing probes, and in 31 of 81 failing-probe attempts, so it does not separate them | gate: red mutant (127.0.0.1:9, 10 probes) recorded 10/10 failed with 0 attempts at stage>=1, plus 10 ebanking probes 10 s apart in one window; not a rule, Fisher p=1 probe-level, per-index p>=0.3 (1 pass is too few for any rule) | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-HS3-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv (98 rows), EBANKING-HS3-STATS-*.txt, chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; stage is effectively 0 or 4 because rustls logs the four markers together, so the 1-3 stages never show; open question, not chased (noisy site = band, no follow-up row): what separates the successful 7th attempt from the failed ones
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a: no user-visible change; ebanking.bdm-sa.com still loads on 3 of 7 a11y-dump probes in one window (rest fail with tls handshake eof after the 8-redial budget), so the live bar is not met | gate: 7 interleaved a11y-dump + openssl s_client probes must all exit 0 with openssl cert on >=3 of 7; RED (3/7 exit 0, 3 consecutive max) | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-RECHECK2-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.tsv rows 1-3 rc 0 (7.0-7.4 s), rows 4-7 rc 1 with 'tls handshake eof' (14.6-15.7 s); openssl cert on 4/7; red mutant 127.0.0.1:9 3/3 rc 1 (EBANKING-RECHECK2-MUTANT tsv); chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116
R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a: no user-visible change; ebanking.bdm-sa.com still fails for manuk on most probes (3228 B on 3 of 3 in the oracle windows, tls handshake eof elsewhere), so the Chrome-finishes-the-handshake question is not answered | gate: not run as written, BLOCKED on the precondition; the default-profile Chrome path that the gate would relay is HTTP/3 over QUIC, which a TCP relay cannot carry (relay logs 0 B); the TCP-only Chrome oracle fails where manuk passes | evidence: docs/sprint/reach/EBANKING-ORACLE-R0.1.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.a.tsv (chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116, Chrome 0 B x3 timeout 120 s with --disable-quic, netlog h2/http1.1 valid; manuk rc=0 3228 B x3); re-check from recorded evidence only, no live probe run this invocation

R0.4.a.c.a.b.a.a.a.a.a.a.b.b: whitehouse.gov's release drive-probe hang is located: navigate never returns (103 s wall, killed), all load phases through "subframes" finish in ~4 s, then the page-fetch pump settles /live.json (377-byte body) and the page's JS continuation inside eval(__deliver) spins with RSS 8-9 GB and no script deadline armed (run_deferred is armed, deliver_bytes is not) | gate: scripts/wh-release-hang-phase.sh green (rc 124, navigate-start without navigate-end, last phase subframes, last log "performing page fetches"); mutants red: example.com, tmo 30, tmo 500, missing binary | evidence: docs/bench/wh-release-hang-20261103.tsv, docs/bench/wh-release-hang-sub-phase-20261103.txt, chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116; follow-up .a (arm deadline, engine/ via tick.sh)

R3.3: an agent can call handoff(reason, timeout_ms); a human acts on the same live page and the agent resumes with an effect diff of what they changed (idle human = 'no change', no resume = TIMED OUT, no human surface = explicit error); the shipped stdio binary has no surface attached yet (R3.3.a) | gate: mcp/tests/handoff.rs scripted-human round trip, mutation (skip human turn) RED | evidence: handoff_round_trips ok; mutated run failed 'diff must show what the human did'; cargo test -p manuk-mcp all green, release build ok
R3.4: a page process that has loaded can now be forked 64 ways (children each act differently, report correct independent a11y digests, distinct Math.random/crypto/TLS ClientHello randoms, script deadline still cuts a spinning child); verified on a ~300-node fixture only, real-page scale is R3.4.a | gate: scripts/r3-4-fork-gate.sh --mutants (N=64, GREEN; mutants naive-fork/no-reseed/leaked-socket all RED) | evidence: 64/64 replied, hung=0, 64 distinct digests (idx 0,31,63 equal a never-forked run), 65 pids -> 65 distinct TLS randoms, rss p50 13.7 MB, private_dirty p50 4.5 MB / p99 6.4 MB, child fork p50 3.1 ms / p99 13.5 ms (docs/bench/r3-4-fork-20261106-114833.tsv, chrome_ver=145.0.7632.116); naive fork hung 8/8
W3.17: the agent now runs one step per window turn instead of freezing the browser: a task shows an AGENT badge, a bottom strip with the step log and Stop / Take over / Resume buttons, Esc halts it before the next step, Take over hands you the live page and Resume tells the agent what you changed; consent and clipboard writes can only come from a real key/mouse event | gate: shell agent_run tests (10-step frame budget, Esc within 1 step, take-over effect diff), manuk-consent compile-fail doctests, shell/tests/w317_clipboard_gate.rs | evidence: release 10-step run frame p95 25.7 ms, max gap 25.7 ms (debug build ~130 ms/step so wall-clock asserts are release-only); Esc after step 3 -> 3 steps; mutations (forge ctor, Esc ignored, empty diff, admit-all clipboard) each turn a gate red; GUI strip/badge drawing compiled but not yet run in a live window (W3.17.a), and a slow LLM call still blocks one turn (W3.17.b)
